# Whole-estate migration impact report — scan of 2026-09-26

Generated by migration-impact-scanner on 2026-09-26 (sandbox clock, UTC). Machine-readable twin: `impact-report.json` (schema_version 1.0). One-page summary: `impact-summary.md`. Auditor workbook: `impact-report.xlsx` (Summary, Alerts, Inventory, CVEs). Raw logs, contract captures, sandbox diffs and scripts: `evidence/`.

**Scope (treated as production):** `spring-petclinic-rest` @ v1.5.2 (commit 6479aaa4b3b47b646e3f4c7d6f4fa293ebd2791d); `spring-petclinic-angular` @ 22935fc04933e4dec3b20bcfb8720f56b09f170d; `petclinic-infra` from the uploaded petclinic-infra.tar.gz. Nothing was pushed or modified outside the sandbox; all experiments ran on throwaway copies under /tmp/builds.

**Method:** inventory from pom.xml + `mvn dependency:list`, package.json + package-lock.json, requirements.txt, runtime.txt, db-inventory.yaml, oracle-profile.xml, .travis.yml, maven-wrapper.properties. Latest versions from Maven Central metadata, npm registry, PyPI JSON, nodejs.org, Adoptium API, api.spring.io, python.org. CVEs from OSV (api.osv.dev; aggregates GitHub Security Advisories + NVD) for all 1,221 installed package versions, and from the Oracle CPU/CSPU risk matrices (Jan-Sep 2026) for Oracle Database and Java SE. CVSS computed from the published vectors (v3.x preferred, v4.0/v2 fallback). **Sandbox:** OpenJDK 17.0.20.1, Temurin 25.0.4.1, Maven 3.9.11, Node 22.22.0 + Node 24.21.0 (SHA-256 verified), CPython 3.14.7 (uv), Playwright Chromium; no Docker daemon.

**Legend:** UNVERIFIED = could not be confirmed from an official source in this run. Every file:line was read from the pinned checkouts (paths are relative to the directory holding the three repositories). Build/run IDs (Bxx/Rxx/Sxx/Xxx) refer to section 5.4 and `evidence/`.

<a id="new-since-last-scan"></a>
## 1. New since last scan

Compared with the previous scan state in `/mnt/memory/scan-history/` (scan_date 2026-09-26; run5 (scan 2026-09-26 ~04:10-05:30 UTC); previous state = run4 of 2026-09-24). This run is **not** a baseline.

- **[new_finding] Oracle Oct 2026 CPU is 24 days away and the 19.3 database is still unpatched: the patch window decision is now urgent** (`oracle-database`) — The Oracle Security Alerts index re-read in this run still lists the Jul 2026 CPU and the Sep 2026 CSPU as the latest advisories (no new CPU, CSPU or Security Alert since the previous scan earlier today). The next CPU is on 2026-10-20. db-inventory.yaml:6-8 still declares 19.3.0.0.0 with no RU and no OJVM patch. If RU 19.32 is not applied before then, the gap grows by another quarterly RU. ([source](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/))
- **[new_security_patch] No newly published CVEs and no new Oracle advisory/security patch since the previous scan** (`oracle-database`) — OSV was re-queried for all 1,221 installed package versions (Maven, npm, PyPI): no advisory ID appears that was absent from the previous state. Oracle: the latest advisories are still the Sep 2026 CSPU (Rev 1, 15 Sep 2026) and the Jul 2026 CPU (Rev 7, 20 Aug 2026); the 2026 Security Alerts (CVE-2026-21992 Identity Manager/OWSM, CVE-2026-35273 PeopleSoft) do not affect this estate. Next Oracle CPU: 20 Oct 2026; next CSPU: 17 Nov 2026. ([source](https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/))
- **[new_release] Pre-releases unchanged since the previous scan; no GA release affects the approved targets** (`spring-boot`) — Maven Central still shows the same pre-releases as the previous scan: spring-boot 4.2.0-M2, spring-framework 7.1.0-M2, spring-security 7.2.0-M2, spring-data 4.2.0-M2 (2026-09-24), Hibernate ORM 8.0.0.Beta3 (2026-09-25) and slf4j 2.1.0-alpha1. The latest official versions stay Spring Boot 4.1.1, Spring Framework 7.0.9, Spring Security 7.1.1, Hibernate ORM 7.4.10.Final, Tomcat 11.0.26, Angular 22.2.0, Node 24.21.0 LTS, Python 3.14.7 and Temurin 25.0.4.1 (Adoptium API). The Logback 1.6.4 override decision from the previous scan is still open. ([source](https://api.spring.io/projects/spring-boot/generations))
- **[correction] Method: new Excel workbook for auditors, and CVSS scores now stored in the scan state** (`oracle-database`) — This run adds impact-report.xlsx (sheets Summary, Alerts, Inventory, CVEs; same data as impact-report.json). The scan state in /mnt/memory/scan-history/ now also stores the CVSS score of every CVE ID per component_key, so the next scan can report re-scored advisories and not only new IDs. The sandbox was empty again at the start of this run, so all build/run evidence (B01-B31, R01-R13, S01, S02, X01) was re-executed from the pinned refs; results are in section 5.4. ([source](https://osv.dev/))
- **[new_release] No latest-version moves since the previous scan** (`angular`) — Registry latest versions re-read this run are unchanged versus the previous state for every tracked component (Spring Boot 4.1.1, Tomcat 11.0.26, Angular 22.2.0, Node 24.21.0 LTS, Python 3.14.7, Temurin 25.0.4.1, ojdbc17 23.26.3.0.0, ...). <a id="alerts"></a>
## 2. Alerts

Criteria: CVSS >= 9.0, database behind the latest Oracle security patch, end-of-life runtimes/frameworks, and blockers found in the target stack. One alert per component_key.

<a id="alert-oracle-database"></a>
### ALERT-01 [CRITICAL] Oracle Database 19c Enterprise Edition (PETCLINIC @ petclinic-db01) 19.3.0.0.0, Release Update applied: none (base release, April 2019) (petclinic-infra)

`oracle-database` — ALERT: DATABASE BEHIND THE LATEST CRITICAL PATCH UPDATE. db-inventory.yaml:6-8 declares 19.3.0.0.0 with no Release Update and no OJVM patch since install (19.3 = April 2019 base release). The latest 19c security level is RU 19.32 (Jul 2026 CPU) + Aug 2026 CSPU + Sep 2026 CSPU DB patches, i.e. ~29 quarterly RUs behind and missing every CPU since Oct 2019, including the 7 Oracle advisories of 2026. 22 DB CVEs (+4 OJVM, +3 JDBC) from the 2026 advisories alone affect 19.3, 5 with CVSS >= 9 (CVE-2026-61211, CVE-2026-71063, CVE-2026-71064, CVE-2026-47040, CVE-2026-71102).

**Fix:** Apply RU 19.32 + OJVM RU 19.32 (Jul 2026 CPU), then the Aug and Sep 2026 CSPU DB patches, run datapatch; schedule the 2026-10-20 CPU. MOS patch numbers UNVERIFIED (login required). Strategic option: 26ai (23.26.x).

Sources: <https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cpujul2026.html> ; <https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspuaug2026.html> ; <https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspusep2026.html> ; <https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/>

<a id="alert-jdk"></a>
### ALERT-02 [CRITICAL] Java runtime (JDK on petclinic-app01) + build target + CI JDK JDK 1.8.0_161 (app server); java.version 1.8 (build); oraclejdk8 (CI) (petclinic-rest)

`jdk` — JDK 8u161 (Jan 2018) runs petclinic-rest: 32 Java SE CVEs from the 2026 Oracle advisories alone affect it (max CVSS 7.8), plus eight years of earlier CPUs. The target framework (Spring Boot 4.1.1) requires Java 17+; Oracle Java 17 Premier Support ended Sep 2026.

**Fix:** Java 25 LTS (Temurin 25.0.4.1) in the same release as Spring Boot 4.1.1 (proven B14). A JDK-only upgrade of the current code is not viable (B01/B02, R01 POST 400).

Sources: <https://api.adoptium.net/v3/info/available_releases> ; <https://www.oracle.com/java/technologies/java-se-support-roadmap.html> ; <https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cspuaug2026.html>

<a id="alert-spring-boot"></a>
### ALERT-03 [CRITICAL] Spring Boot (spring-boot, -autoconfigure, starters) 1.5.2.RELEASE (petclinic-rest)

`spring-boot` — Spring Boot 1.5.x is end-of-life (OSS ended 2019-08-31, commercial 2020-11-30); 6 advisories on Boot itself and it pins the whole vulnerable stack below.

**Fix:** Spring Boot 4.1.1 via 2.7.18 -> 3.5.16 (transit steps). Main code compiles and runs on 4.1.1 after the listed changes (B12, R05, B14).

Sources: <https://api.spring.io/projects/spring-boot/generations> ; <https://github.com/spring-projects/spring-boot/wiki/Spring-Boot-4.0-Migration-Guide>

<a id="alert-spring-framework"></a>
### ALERT-04 [CRITICAL] Spring Framework (core, beans, context, expression, web, webmvc) 4.3.7.RELEASE (petclinic-rest)

`spring-framework` — Spring Framework 4.3.7 (EOL 2020-12-31): 36 advisories, 2 with CVSS >= 9 incl. CVE-2022-22965 "Spring4Shell" (9.8) and CVE-2016-1000027 (9.8).

**Fix:** Spring Framework 7.0.9 via Boot 4.1.1.

Sources: <https://api.spring.io/projects/spring-framework/generations> ; <https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2022-22965>

<a id="alert-spring-security"></a>
### ALERT-05 [CRITICAL] Spring Security (core, web) 4.2.2.RELEASE (petclinic-rest)

`spring-security` — Spring Security 4.2.2: 12 advisories, 2 with CVSS >= 9 (e.g. CVE-2022-22978 9.8).

**Fix:** Spring Security 7.1.1 via Boot 4.1.1; rewrite BasicAuthenticationAdapter as SecurityFilterChain (proven B12).

Sources: <https://repo1.maven.org/maven2/org/springframework/security/spring-security-core/maven-metadata.xml>

<a id="alert-apache-tomcat"></a>
### ALERT-06 [CRITICAL] Apache Tomcat embedded (core, websocket; provided, executable WAR) 8.5.11 (petclinic-rest)

`apache-tomcat` — Embedded Tomcat 8.5.11 (8.5.x EOL 2024-03-31): 58 advisories, 13 with CVSS >= 9 (e.g. CVE-2020-1938 Ghostcat, CVE-2025-24813). Target pitfall: Spring Boot 4.1.1 manages tomcat-embed-core 11.0.24, affected by CVE-2026-65905 (9.8), CVE-2026-65182 and CVE-2026-68525 (fixed 11.0.25) — S01.

**Fix:** Upgrade with Boot 4.1.1 AND pin <tomcat.version>11.0.26</tomcat.version> (proven: "Starting Servlet engine: [Apache Tomcat/11.0.26]", R03-R05, B14).

Sources: <https://tomcat.apache.org/security-11.html> ; <https://tomcat.apache.org/whichversion.html> ; <https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-65905>

<a id="alert-jackson-databind"></a>
### ALERT-07 [CRITICAL] Jackson databind 2.8.7 (petclinic-rest)

`jackson-databind` — jackson-databind 2.8.7: 56 advisories, 23 with CVSS >= 9 (polymorphic deserialization gadget chains).

**Fix:** Jackson 3.2.x with Boot 4.1.1 after porting the custom serializers; interim Jackson 2 line 2.22.3 via the spring-boot-jackson2 bridge (proven contract-identical, R05/R09).

Sources: <https://repo1.maven.org/maven2/com/fasterxml/jackson/core/jackson-databind/maven-metadata.xml>

<a id="alert-python"></a>
### ALERT-08 [CRITICAL] CPython runtime for ops/healthcheck.py (petclinic-app01) 3.6.8 (petclinic-infra)

`python` — Python 3.6.8 is end-of-life since 2021-12-23 (no security fixes); none of the current library releases support it.

**Fix:** CPython 3.14.7 with the latest libraries (install proven B26; healthcheck needs 4 code changes, R07).

Sources: <https://devguide.python.org/versions/> ; <https://peps.python.org/pep-0494/>

<a id="alert-pyyaml"></a>
### ALERT-09 [CRITICAL] PyYAML (PyPI) 3.12 (petclinic-infra)

`pyyaml` — PyYAML 3.12: CVE-2017-18342 and CVE-2020-14343 (9.8, arbitrary code execution via yaml.load) — healthcheck.py:20 calls exactly yaml.load(f) without a Loader.

**Fix:** PyYAML 6.0.3 and yaml.safe_load (proven R07).

Sources: <https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2020-14343> ; <https://github.com/yaml/pyyaml/wiki/PyYAML-yaml.load(input)-Deprecation>

<a id="alert-angular"></a>
### ALERT-10 [CRITICAL] Angular framework (@angular/core, common, compiler, forms, router, animations, platform-*) 6.1.6 (petclinic-angular)

`angular` — Angular 6.1.6 is end-of-life (v2-v19 unsupported): 17 advisories on @angular/core/common/compiler (max CVSS 8.8).

**Fix:** Angular 22.2.0 on Node 24 LTS (build proven B28-B30; UI proven R12). Requires Eager change detection + provideZoneChangeDetection().

Sources: <https://angular.dev/reference/releases> ; <https://angular.dev/update-guide>

<a id="alert-nodejs"></a>
### ALERT-11 [CRITICAL] Node.js (CI build runtime) 10.10 (petclinic-angular)

`nodejs` — CI builds the front-end on Node.js 10.10 (EOL 2021-04-30); the current toolchain cannot even install on a current Node (node-sass needs python2, B15).

**Fix:** Node.js 24.21.0 LTS (Angular 22 engines ^22.22.3 || ^24.15.0 || >=26).

Sources: <https://github.com/nodejs/Release/blob/main/schedule.json> ; <https://nodejs.org/dist/index.json>

<a id="alert-apache-maven"></a>
### ALERT-18 [CRITICAL] Apache Maven via Maven Wrapper 3.3.3 (petclinic-rest)

`apache-maven` — The Maven Wrapper pins Maven 3.3.3 (.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.properties:1): CVE-2021-26291 (9.1); also too old for Spring Boot 4.1.1 plugins (B27 "requires Maven version 3.6.3").

**Fix:** distributionUrl -> Maven 3.9.16.

Sources: <https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2021-26291> ; <https://repo1.maven.org/maven2/org/apache/maven/apache-maven/maven-metadata.xml>

<a id="alert-ojdbc"></a>
### ALERT-19 [HIGH] Oracle JDBC driver (oracle Maven profile merged into petclinic-rest) com.oracle:ojdbc6 11.2.0.4 (petclinic-infra)

`ojdbc` — ojdbc6 11.2.0.4 is not in Oracle's JDBC interoperability matrix for Database 19.x, supports JDK 6-8 only (cannot run on the Java 17/25 target) and is not on Maven Central (B04). JDBC CVEs CVE-2026-47045, CVE-2026-47060, CVE-2026-47061 (Jul 2026 CPU).

**Fix:** com.oracle.database.jdbc:ojdbc17:23.26.3.0.0.

Sources: <https://www.oracle.com/database/technologies/faq-jdbc.html> ; <https://www.oracle.com/security-alerts/cpujul2026.html>

<a id="alert-hibernate-oracle-dialect"></a>
### ALERT-20 [HIGH] Hibernate dialect for Oracle (configuration) org.hibernate.dialect.Oracle10gDialect (petclinic-infra)

`hibernate-oracle-dialect` — org.hibernate.dialect.Oracle10gDialect no longer exists in Hibernate 6/7: Boot 4.1.1 fails at startup with StrategySelectionException (R06).

**Fix:** Remove spring.jpa.database-platform (auto-detection) or use org.hibernate.dialect.OracleDialect.

Sources: <https://docs.jboss.org/hibernate/orm/6.0/migration-guide/migration-guide.html#dialects>

<a id="alert-oracle-linux"></a>
### ALERT-21 [HIGH] Oracle Linux (DB host petclinic-db01) 7.6 (petclinic-infra)

`oracle-linux` — Oracle Linux 7.6 on the DB host: OL7 Premier Support ended Dec 2024 (Extended Support to Jul 2029, separate subscription). Errata level UNVERIFIED.

**Fix:** Plan OL9/OL10 in the DB re-platforming window; confirm RU 19.32 certification in MOS (UNVERIFIED).

Sources: <https://www.oracle.com/a/ocom/docs/elsp-lifetime-069338.pdf>

<a id="alert-spring-retry"></a>
### ALERT-22 [MEDIUM] Spring Retry (transitive) 1.2.0.RELEASE (Boot 4.1.1 target would resolve 1.0.3.RELEASE via spring-data-jdbc-core, S01) (petclinic-rest)

`spring-retry` — Target-stack blocker: Boot 4 no longer manages Spring Retry, so the abandoned spring-data-jdbc-core 1.2.1 (pom.xml:83-93) drags spring-retry 1.0.3.RELEASE (CVE-2026-41710) into the Boot 4.1.1 build (S01).

**Fix:** Remove spring-data-jdbc-core and rewrite JdbcPetVisitExtractor (JdbcPetVisitExtractor.java:18,30), or pin spring-retry 2.0.13.

Sources: <https://github.com/spring-projects/spring-boot/wiki/Spring-Boot-4.0-Migration-Guide> ; <https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-41710>

## 4. CVEs affecting the current versions

Unique IDs — **production scope: 332** (CRITICAL 53, HIGH 129, MEDIUM 124, LOW 26, unscored 0); test scope: 5 (CRITICAL 0, HIGH 2, MEDIUM 3, LOW 0); build toolchain: 276 (CRITICAL 32, HIGH 119, MEDIUM 100, LOW 11, unscored 14).

"fixed in" = first fixed release above the installed version (OSV ranges) or, for Oracle, the RU/CSPU derived from the risk-matrix "supported versions affected" column. The recommended target is always the latest version in section 3. IDs without a CVE alias are listed by GHSA/OSV ID.

<a id="imp-rest-jdk"></a>
#### IMP-REST-JDK: JDK 1.8.0_161 -> 17/25 under the CURRENT code (Spring Boot 1.5.2) — feasibility check

- **component_key:** `jdk`
- **Target:** not viable as a standalone step: the JDK must move together with Spring Boot 4.1.1
- **Mandatory intermediate steps:** none

| # | breaking change affecting this code | file:line | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Spring 4.3 CGLIB proxies need ClassLoader.defineClass: 168 of 169 tests error with InaccessibleObjectException on JDK 17 (B01) | `spring-petclinic-rest/pom.xml:17` | [doc](https://www.oracle.com/java/technologies/java-se-support-roadmap.html) |
| 2 | maven-war-plugin 2.6 inherited from the Boot 1.5.2 parent fails on JDK 17 (XStream reflection on java.util.TreeMap) unless MAVEN_OPTS --add-opens (B02/B03) | `spring-petclinic-rest/pom.xml:9` | [doc](https://www.oracle.com/java/technologies/java-se-support-roadmap.html) |
| 3 | Hibernate Validator 5.3.4 on JDK 17: POST /api/owners with a valid body -> 400 "HV000041: Call to TraversableResolver.isReachable() threw an exception" (R01; Boot 4.1.1 returns 201). Root cause not isolated (UNVERIFIED) | `spring-petclinic-rest/src/main/java/org/springframework/samples/petclinic/rest/OwnerRestController.java:89` | [doc](https://hibernate.org/validator/releases/) |

<a id="imp-rest-boot4"></a>
#### IMP-REST-BOOT4: Spring Boot 1.5.2.RELEASE -> 4.1.1 (Spring Framework 7.0.9, Security 7.1.1, Hibernate 7.4, Jackson 3) on Java 25

- **component_key:** `spring-boot`
- **Target:** Spring Boot 4.1.1 + Java 25 LTS (Temurin 25.0.4.1), <tomcat.version>11.0.26</tomcat.version>, springdoc-openapi 3.1.1, jakarta.xml.bind-api, spring-boot-jackson2 (interim)
- **Mandatory intermediate steps:** 2.7.18 (last 2.x; the Boot 3.0 migration guide requires moving to 2.7 first; javax namespace; compiled in B05) -> 3.5.16 (latest 3.5.x; the Boot 4.0 guide requires 3.5 first; jakarta; compiled in B06/B07) -> 4.1.1 (B08-B14)

<a id="imp-ng-22"></a>
#### IMP-NG-22: Angular 6.1.6 -> 22.2.0 (RxJS 7.8.2, TypeScript 6.0, zone.js 0.16.3, Node 24 LTS)

- **component_key:** `angular`
- **Target:** Angular 22.2.0 (+ CDK/Material 22.2.0), @angular/build 22.2.0 application builder, RxJS 7.8.2, TypeScript 6.0.x, zone.js 0.16.3, Node 24.21.0 LTS, Bootstrap 5.3.8, moment 2.31.0 (or a maintained date library)
- **Mandatory intermediate steps:** ng update one major at a time 7 -> 8 -> ... -> 21 -> 22 so each version's automatic migrations run (v22 migration adds ChangeDetectionStrategy.Eager; v19 adds standalone:false) -> switch Node per CLI major (10 -> 12/14 -> 16/18 -> 20 -> 22.22.3+/24) -> remove rxjs-compat after the RxJS 6 import clean-up, before RxJS 7

- **Target:** CPython 3.14.7; requests 2.34.2, urllib3 2.8.0, PyYAML 6.0.3, Jinja2 3.1.6, oracledb 26.0.1 (Thin mode, no Oracle Client)

|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Runtime declared as python-3.6.8 (EOL 2021-12-23; also db-inventory.yaml:14) | `petclinic-infra/ops/runtime.txt:1` | [doc](https://peps.python.org/pep-0494/) |
| 2 | requests 2.19.1 / urllib3 1.23 install but cannot be imported on 3.14: "No module named 'urllib3.packages.six.moves'" (B25); latest require Python >= 3.10 | `petclinic-infra/ops/requirements.txt:1` | [doc](https://pypi.org/project/requests/) |

| 6 | import cx_Oracle -> ModuleNotFoundError with the latest libraries (R07); use import oracledb | `petclinic-infra/ops/healthcheck.py:10` | [doc](https://python-oracledb.readthedocs.io/en/latest/user_guide/appendix_c.html) |

#### IMP-INFRA-JDBC: Oracle JDBC ojdbc6 11.2.0.4 + Oracle10gDialect -> ojdbc17 23.26.3.0.0 + auto-detected OracleDialect

- **component_key:** `ojdbc`
- **Target:** com.oracle.database.jdbc:ojdbc17:23.26.3.0.0; remove spring.jpa.database-platform
- **Mandatory intermediate steps:** none

| # | breaking change affecting this code | file:line | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | com.oracle:ojdbc6:11.2.0.4 is not on Maven Central -> "Could not find artifact com.oracle:ojdbc6:jar:11.2.0.4" when the profile is merged (B04); ojdbc6 = JDK 6-8 only | `petclinic-infra/maven/oracle-profile.xml:7` | [doc](https://www.oracle.com/database/technologies/faq-jdbc.html) |
| 2 | org.hibernate.dialect.Oracle10gDialect does not exist in Hibernate 7 -> StrategySelectionException at startup (R06) | `petclinic-infra/maven/oracle-profile.xml:13` | [doc](https://docs.jboss.org/hibernate/orm/6.0/migration-guide/migration-guide.html#dialects) |
| 3 | The profile sets spring.datasource.url / spring.jpa.database-platform as MAVEN <properties>; application.properties has no @...@ placeholders, so they do not reach Spring at runtime (how production injects them is UNVERIFIED) | `petclinic-infra/maven/oracle-profile.xml:11` | [doc](https://docs.spring.io/spring-boot/how-to/properties-and-configuration.html#howto.properties-and-configuration.expand-properties.maven) |
| 4 | Entities use GenerationType.IDENTITY while the Oracle DDL defines sequences and plain NUMBER(10) keys (schema.sql:2-5, 8) -> JPA inserts on Oracle would fail unless triggers exist (static analysis; no Oracle DB in sandbox, UNVERIFIED) | `spring-petclinic-rest/src/main/java/org/springframework/samples/petclinic/model/BaseEntity.java:34` | [doc](https://docs.jboss.org/hibernate/orm/6.0/migration-guide/migration-guide.html#dialects) |
| 5 | Oracle schema has no users/roles tables but BasicAuthenticationAdapter.java:38-39 queries them -> enabling basic auth on Oracle would fail (static analysis, UNVERIFIED) | `petclinic-infra/oracle/schema.sql:7` | [doc](https://www.oracle.com/database/technologies/faq-jdbc.html) |

<a id="cross-module"></a>
## 6. Cross-module dependencies and migration order

| from | to | constraint | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| petclinic-angular | petclinic-rest | REST contract: base URL http://<host>:9966/petclinic/api/ (environment.prod.ts:25, environment.ts:26) + owners/pets/vets/visits/pettypes/specialties (e.g. owner.service.ts:35); nested owner<->pets JSON and dates "yyyy/MM/dd" (pet-add.component.ts:73). The backend upgrade must keep the context path, anonymous access and the Jackson 2 serializer output; CORS via @CrossOrigin (OwnerRestController.java:47). | R01 baseline, R03 (context path "/" + 401), R13 (Angular 6 fails on Jackson 3 output), R09/R12 (Angular 6 and 22 both work against Boot 4 + Jackson 2 bridge) |
| petclinic-infra (ops/healthcheck.py) | petclinic-rest | Same REST contract: healthcheck.yaml:1 -> /petclinic/api/, len() of the JSON lists from /owners and /vets (healthcheck.py:28-29), no credentials. | R08 |
| petclinic-infra (ops/healthcheck.py) | Oracle DB PETCLINIC | cx_Oracle 6.4.1 cannot be imported on Python 3.14 (B25); python-oracledb Thin mode needs no Oracle Client and supports Oracle Database 12.1+ (python-oracledb docs), so it works with 19c before and after patching; queries PETCLINIC.OWNERS/VETS (healthcheck.py:38) which exist in schema.sql:7,18. | B25, R07, https://python-oracledb.readthedocs.io/en/latest/user_guide/installation.html |
| petclinic-rest (oracle profile from petclinic-infra) | Oracle DB PETCLINIC 19c | JDBC driver must be listed for DB 19.x in the Oracle interoperability matrix (JDBC 23.x/21.x/19.x; 11.2 not listed) and match the JDK (ojdbc6 = JDK 6-8; ojdbc17 = JDK 17+ incl. 25). Dialect must be Hibernate-7-compatible (OracleDialect). | B04, R06, https://www.oracle.com/database/technologies/faq-jdbc.html |
| petclinic-rest | petclinic-infra (app-server JDK) | db-inventory.yaml:13 declares JDK 1.8.0_161 for the WAR; Boot 4.1.1 needs Java 17+ (B09) and runs on Temurin 25.0.4.1 (B14). The app-server JDK must be upgraded in the same release as the Boot 4 WAR; the current WAR is not viable on JDK 17+ (B01, R01). | B09, B14, R01 |
| petclinic-infra (app-server Python) | petclinic-infra (ops script) | db-inventory.yaml:14 declares Python 3.6.8 while current requests/urllib3/oracledb require >= 3.10 -> host Python and requirements.txt move together. | B24-B26 |
| petclinic-rest (JPA entities) | petclinic-infra/oracle/schema.sql | Entities use IDENTITY generation (BaseEntity.java:34) and the HSQLDB DDL uses IDENTITY columns (initDB.sql:13), but the Oracle DDL uses sequences without identity/triggers and lacks users/roles tables — pre-existing mismatch to fix while touching the Oracle profile (static analysis, UNVERIFIED at runtime). | static analysis |
| petclinic-rest (build/CI) | build toolchain | Maven Wrapper 3.3.3 (.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.properties:1) and CI oraclejdk8 (.travis.yml:2) must be upgraded (Maven >= 3.6.3, JDK 17+/25) before or with the Boot 3/4 steps. | B27 |
| petclinic-angular (build) | CI Node runtime | .travis.yml:5 pins Node 10.10; Angular 22.2 requires ^22.22.3 \|\| ^24.15.0 \|\| >=26; the sandbox default Node 22.22.0 is below that floor (Node 24.21.0 used). | B15-B30 |

<a id="migration-order"></a>
**Migration order imposed by these dependencies:**

| step | action | component_keys | depends on |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Oracle DB security patching now, independent of code: RU 19.32 + OJVM + Aug/Sep 2026 CSPU on PETCLINIC, then the 2026-10-20 CPU. Regression-test connectivity of the current ojdbc6 11.2 client (already an unsupported combination; UNVERIFIED). | `oracle-database`, `oracle-ojvm` | - |
| 2 | Ops script: Python 3.14.7 + latest libs + python-oracledb and the 4 healthcheck.py fixes (works against the current backend, R08). | `python`, `requests`, `urllib3`, `pyyaml`, `jinja2`, `cx-oracle` | - |
| 3 | Freeze the REST contract: turn the captured 1.5.2 responses (evidence/contract-asis-1.5.2/) into contract tests shared by petclinic-rest, petclinic-angular and the ops script; add a UI smoke test (R09-R12 style). | `spring-boot`, `angular` | - |
| 4 | Build toolchain: Maven Wrapper -> 3.9.16, CI JDK -> 25, remove jcenter; CI Node -> 24 LTS. | `apache-maven`, `jdk`, `nodejs` | - |
| 5 | petclinic-rest transit step: Spring Boot 2.7.18 on JDK 17 (properties renames, validation starter, web.servlet.support, drop orm.hibernate3 import, mysql coordinates). Not deployed. | `spring-boot` | 3, 4 |
| 6 | petclinic-rest transit step: Spring Boot 3.5.16 (javax -> jakarta, SecurityFilterChain, springfox -> springdoc, JUnit 5). Not deployed (OSS ended 2026-06-30). | `spring-boot`, `spring-security`, `springfox`, `junit` | 5 |
| 7 | petclinic-rest + app-server JDK + JDBC in ONE release: Spring Boot 4.1.1 on Temurin 25.0.4.1, tomcat.version 11.0.26, ojdbc17 23.26.3.0.0, dialect auto-detection, spring-data-jdbc-core removed, Jackson 2 bridge (contract-identical, R05; current Angular 6 UI keeps working, R09). | `spring-boot`, `spring-framework`, `apache-tomcat`, `jdk`, `ojdbc`, `hibernate-oracle-dialect`, `jackson-databind`, `spring-retry` | 1, 6 |
| 8 | petclinic-angular -> 22.2.0 on Node 24 LTS (ng update per major; keep Eager CD + zone.js; Bootstrap 5; remove legacy devDependencies). Can be developed in parallel; deploy after step 7 because it is validated against the Boot 4 backend (R12). | `angular`, `angular-material`, `rxjs`, `zone-js`, `typescript`, `bootstrap`, `jquery`, `moment` | 4, 7 |
| 9 | petclinic-rest: port the Jackson custom (de)serializers to Jackson 3 and drop the deprecated spring-boot-jackson2 bridge; re-run the contract + UI tests against Angular 22 and the ops script. | `jackson-databind` | 7, 8 |
| 10 | Platform: Oracle Linux 7.6 -> 9/10 for petclinic-db01 (and evaluate 26ai) in a dedicated window; certification UNVERIFIED. | `oracle-linux`, `oracle-database` | 1 |

<a id="decisions"></a>
### Decisions needed from approvers

- Java target: approve Java 25 LTS (Temurin 25.0.4.1) rather than 17 (Oracle Java 17 Premier Support ended Sep 2026), shipped in the same release as Spring Boot 4.1.1.
- Spring Boot path: approve 1.5.2 -> 2.7.18 -> 3.5.16 -> 4.1.1 with 2.7/3.5 as non-deployed transit steps (both out of OSS support).
- Version overrides: pin Tomcat 11.0.26 over the Boot-managed 11.0.24 (mandatory, 3 CRITICAL CVEs); decide whether to also override Hibernate 7.4.5 -> 7.4.10, Hibernate Validator 9.1.3 -> 9.1.4, Jackson 2.21.5/3.1.5 -> 2.22.3/3.2.3, Logback 1.5.38 -> 1.6.4 (published 2026-09-24; still open).
- Accept the deprecated spring-boot-jackson2 bridge as a temporary measure (proven contract-identical for Angular and ops) until serializers are ported to Jackson 3.
- Oracle DB patch window for RU 19.32 + OJVM + Aug/Sep 2026 CSPU (OJVM/datapatch downtime) before the 2026-10-20 CPU; stay on 19c LTR vs plan 26ai.
- Angular: incremental ng update through 16 majors vs re-scaffold on Angular 22; keep Eager change detection + zone.js initially (required, R10-R12) vs invest in OnPush/signals; accept Bootstrap 3 -> 5 visual changes and a moment.js replacement.
- Make a UI smoke test a release gate for the front-end upgrade (a green `ng build` shipped an empty UI in R10).
- Oracle Linux 7.6: fund Extended Support until the OS migration, or schedule OL9/OL10.
